{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"broaches","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"broaching","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"broached","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"broached","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To make a hole in, especially a cask of liquor, and put in a tap in order to draw the liquid."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"French knights at Agincourt were unable to broach the English line.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To open, to make an opening into; to pierce."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I broached the subject of contraceptives carefully when the teenager mentioned his promiscuity.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To begin discussion about (something)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"spring"}],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/bɹəʊtʃ/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/bɹoʊt͡ʃ/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":204970,"upstream_offset":988607575,"upstream_record_sha256":"dd5e73304b065246665aa7c5e94f26b013c494efbcb8c8792bd3272ff134645d","word":"broach"}

{"forms":[{"form":"rods","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The circus strong man proved his strength by bending an iron rod, and then straightening it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A straight, round stick, shaft, bar, cane, or staff."]},{"glosses":["A longitudinal pole used for forming part of a framework such as an awning or tent."]},{"examples":[{"text":"When I hooked a snake and not a fish, I got so scared I dropped my rod in the water.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A long slender usually tapering pole used for angling; fishing rod."],"topics":["fishing","hobbies","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["A stick, pole, or bundle of switches or twigs (such as a birch), used for personal defense or to administer corporal punishment by whipping."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The judge imposed on the thief a sentence of fifteen strokes with the rod.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An implement resembling and/or supplanting a rod (particularly a cane) that is used for corporal punishment, and metonymically called the rod, regardless of its actual shape and composition."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I notched a rod and used it to measure the length of rope to cut.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A stick used to measure distance, by using its established length or task-specific temporary marks along its length, or by dint of specific graduated marks."]},{"glosses":["A unit of length equal to 1 pole, a perch, ¹⁄₄ chain, 5+¹⁄₂ yards, 16+¹⁄₂ feet, or exactly 5.0292 meters (these being all equivalent)."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"glosses":["An implement held vertically and viewed through an optical surveying instrument such as a transit, used to measure distance in land surveying and construction layout; an engineer's rod, surveyor's rod, surveying rod, leveling rod, ranging rod. The modern (US) engineer's or surveyor's rod commonly is eight or ten feet long and often designed to extend higher. In former times a surveyor's rod often was a single wooden pole or composed of multiple sectioned and socketed pieces, and besides serving as a sighting target was used to measure distance on the ground horizontally, hence for convenience was of one rod or pole in length, that is, 5+¹⁄₂ yards."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The house had a small yard of about six rods in size.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A unit of area equal to a square rod, 30+¹⁄₄ square yards or ¹⁄₁₆₀ acre."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The engine threw a rod, and then went to pieces before our eyes, springs and coils shooting in all directions.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A straight bar that unites moving parts of a machine, for holding parts together as a connecting rod or for transferring power as a driveshaft."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The rods are more sensitive than the cones, but do not discern color.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A rod cell: a rod-shaped cell in the eye that is sensitive to light."],"topics":["anatomy","medicine","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He applied a gram positive stain, looking for rods indicative of Listeria.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any of a number of long, slender microorganisms."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A stirring rod: a glass rod, typically about 6 inches to 1 foot long and ¹⁄₈ to ¹⁄₄ inch in diameter that can be used to stir liquids in flasks or beakers."],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A pistol; a gun."],"tags":["slang"]},{"glosses":["The penis."],"tags":["slang","vulgar"]},{"glosses":["A hot rod, an automobile or other passenger motor vehicle modified to run faster and often with exterior cosmetic alterations, especially one based originally on a pre-1940s model or (currently) denoting any older vehicle thus modified."],"tags":["slang"]},{"glosses":["A rod-shaped object that appears in photographs or videos traveling at high speed, not seen by the person recording the event, often associated with extraterrestrial entities."],"synonyms":[{"word":"skyfish"}],"topics":["human-sciences","mysticism","mythology","philosophy","sciences","ufology"]},{"glosses":["A Cuisenaire rod."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A coupling rod or connecting rod, which links the driving wheels of a steam locomotive, and some diesel shunters and early electric locomotives."],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"glosses":["A drain rod, being a set of segmented rods with interlocking connectors designed to remain attached even under rotation in use."],"topics":["business","construction","manufacturing","plumbing"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹɒd/","tags":["Northern-England","Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɔd/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ɾɔd/","tags":["Scotland","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ɾɒd/","tags":["UK","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɑd/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɒd/","tags":["Canada"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɔd/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɒd/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[ɹɔ̟d] ~ [ɹɞ̠d]","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":32075,"upstream_offset":217726119,"upstream_record_sha256":"522da00e704888deb434c10179e8d918b835fa2880a0bd5211694fb16175b9df","word":"rod"}
